AT FULL THRUST, THE BRAKES MUST PREVENT MOVEMENT OF THE AIRCRAFT. THE PROTECTIVE PAINT WILL PREVENT CORROSION FOR SIX MONTHS. MAKE SURE THAT THE HOLES IN THE BUSHING ALIGN WITH THOSE IN THE HOUSING. PUT THE SLING ON THE SHACKLE. CONNECT THE TOWING ARM TO THE NOSE GEAR. ADJUST THE SLING UNTIL THE FLAP IS HORIZONTAL. THE VALVE IS INSTALLED HORIZONTALLY. DRY THE SURFACE WITH HOT AIR. THE MODULE CONTAINS FIVE COMPONENTS. PARAGRAPH 7.0 TELLS YOU HOW TO INSTALL THE UNIT. THE DRYING TIME IS USUALLY 24 HOURS. BUT THE PROCEDURE IN THIS STEP WILL DECREASE THE DRYING TIME TO 12 HOURS.

9 Give the limits.

###### STE EXAMPLE

IF YOU INSTALL THE UPPER DRAG STRUT WITH THE HEAD DOWN, IT WILL HIT THE LOWER DRAG STRUT DURING RETRACTION. MAKE SURE THAT THE RODS CANNOT CATCH ON THE FLAPS. MAKE SURE THAT THE LOCKING WIRE DOES NOT TOUCH THE LEVER. MAKE SURE THAT YOU DO NOT HEAR INTERFERENCE IN THE 400 Hz BAND. INTERFERENCE BOLTS ATTACH THE STRAP AND THE BOTTOM SKIN PANEL TO THE TRIFORM MEMBER. EXAMINE THE TANK INTERNALLY. APPLY A SMALL QUANTITY OF LUBRICANT TO THE INTERNAL SIDE OF THE VALVE HOUSING. PUT THE LEVER IN THE MIDDLE POSITION. WHEN THE INPUT LEVER IS BETWEEN 5 AND 10 mm, MEASURE THE PISTON EXTENSION.

###### Non-STE example

Placing the upper drag strut upside down causes interference with the lower drag strut.

Make sure that there is no interference between the rods and the flaps. Ensure that there is no interference between the locking wire and the lever.

Examine the interior of the tank. Apply a small quantity of lubricant to the interior of the valve housing.

Put the lever in intermediate position. When the input lever is in an intermediate position, measure the piston extension.

###### STE EXAMPLE

MAKE THE BASIC ELECTROLYTE NEUTRAL WITH VINEGAR OR BORIC ACID. DO NOT LET THE LEVEL IN THE TANK DECREASE TO LESS THAN HALF. INSTALL A NEW GASKET ON THE FLANGE. REMOVE THE UNIT BEFORE THE NEXT OPERATING CYCLE. HOLD THE FLANGE IN ITS POSITION. THEN, INSTALL THE BOLT. THE PUMP IS ADJACENT TO THE CENTRAL RIB. MAKE SURE THAT THERE IS NO FUEL LEAKAGE. DOES THE LIGHT COME ON? YES OR NO? WHILE THE ENGINE CONTINUES TO TURN, LISTEN FOR UNUSUAL NOISES. IF THERE IS NOISE WHEN THE PUMP OPERATES, EXAMINE THE FILTER FOR UNWANTED PARTICLES. IF NONE OF THE BRACKETS ARE DAMAGED, CONTINUE THE INSPECTION.

###### Non-STE example

Neutralize the basic electrolyte with vinegar or boric acid.

Never allow the level in the tank to drop below half.

Hold the flange in its position. Next, install the bolt. The pump is next to the central rib.

If pump operation is noisy, check the pump filter for unwanted particles.

EXAMINE THE CASTING FOR CORROSION AGAIN. DO A LEAKAGE CHECK OF THE FILTER ASSEMBLY AGAIN. REPAIR THE DAMAGED FABRIC AREA. ATTACH THE TIEDOWN CORD TO A CLEAT. AFTER SERVICING, CLOSE THE COMPARTMENT AGAIN.

###### STE EXAMPLE

THE TURNBUCKLE GOES THROUGH A HOLE IN THE BULKHEAD. THE LEVER MOVES THROUGH A 30-DEG. ARC. APPLY WAX TO THE SURFACE. BE CAREFUL WHEN YOU TOUCH PRINTED CIRCUIT BOARDS. HEAT CAUSES THE PRESSURE TO INCREASE. THIS SIGNAL STARTS THE OPERATION OF THE TEST SET. CUT THE PATCH TO THE CORRECT DIMENSION. OPEN THE CIRCUIT BREAKER. THE HOLD-ON SWITCH RELEASES. AN IRREGULAR SURFACE CAN CAUSE YOU TO FALL. DO THE TROUBLESHOOTING PROCEDURE ON THE VHF NAVIGATION SYSTEM. MAKE SURE THAT THE VALUE SHOWN ON THE INDICATOR IS CORRECT. MAKE SURE THAT THE INDICATION ON THE GAUGE AGREES WITH THE QUANTITY OF FUEL THAT IS IN THE TANK.

###### Non-STE example

The turnbuckle travels through a hole in the bulkhead. The lever travels through a 30-deg. arc. Treat the surface with wax. Be careful when you treat printed circuit boards. Heat triggers a pressure increase. This signal triggers test set operation. Trim the patch to fit.

Trip the circuit breaker. Hold-on switch trips. You can trip and fall on an irregular surface. Troubleshoot the VHF navigation system.

Make sure that the indicator reading is true.

Make sure that the gauge shows the true quantity of fuel that is in the tank.

###### STE EXAMPLE

PUT THE OHMMETER ON A FLAT SURFACE. WHEN YOU SET THE LEVER TO "UP," THE HYDRAULIC PRESSURE MUST DECREASE. FILL THE CONTAINER TO THE MAXIMUM LEVEL. THE AIRFLOW THEN GOES ALONG THE TOP AND BOTTOM SURFACES OF THE SLATS. INSTALL THE BOLTS WITH THEIR HEADS UP. INSTALL THE BOLTS WITH THEIR HEADS UP. DISCONNECT THE UPSTREAM CONNECTION OF THE VALVE. MOVE THE PROBE UPSTREAM AND MONITOR THE TEMPERATURE AGAIN. DISCONNECT THE CONNECTION UPSTREAM OF THE VALVE. UNTIL THIS STEP, THE UNIT IGNORES ALL OUTPUT SIGNALS. DO STEPS 1 THRU 4. YOU CAN INSTALL A MAXIMUM OF TEN WASHERS. MOVE THE LEVER UP. Move the lever upward.

###### Non-STE example

Put the ohmmeter upon a flat surface. Upon UP selection, the hydraulic pressure must decrease.

Fill the container to the upper level. The airflow then passes over the upper and lower surfaces of the slats.

Install the bolts with their heads in uppermost position. Install the bolts with their heads uppermost.

Up to this step the unit ignores all output signals. Do all steps up to and including step 4. You can install up to ten washers.
